\node[caption] at (1.94,20.80) {观测亮度};
\node[caption] at (6.34,20.80) {估计反照率};
\begin{scope}[shift={(.73,17.5)},scale=.96]\Terrain\end{scope}
\node[caption,text width=7.3cm] at (4.25,16.95)
  {高程、局部起伏与高程锚点};

\draw[line] (.4,16.35)--(8.1,16.35);
\node[anchor=west,text=blue,font=\fontsize{12.5}{17}\selectfont\bfseries] at (.45,15.88)
  {提供联合估计的输入与初值};
\node[note,text width=7.4cm,font=\fontsize{10}{14.5}\selectfont] at (.5,15.32)
  {\textbf{当前模型：}高程、反照率和几何。\\
   坐标系、原点、坐标轴、间距、有效域。\\[4pt]
   \textbf{原始观测：}图像亮度、同名像点。\\
   控制测量、相机、太阳方向及曝光。\\[4pt]
   \textbf{空间约束：}邻接、重叠对应与锚点。\\
   保存锚点误差和高程参考面。\\[4pt]
   \textbf{观测模型：}投影、反射及归一化。\\
   坡度与法向由高程差分得到。};
\fill[blue!5,rounded corners=2mm] (.35,9.25) rectangle(8.15,10.55);
\node[caption,text width=7.1cm] at (4.25,9.92)
  {网格与反照率描述“估计是什么”；\\右侧统计状态描述“还不确定什么”。};
\draw[flow,draw=blue] (8.56,25.67)--(9.08,25.67);

% Dominant joint-estimation panel.
\draw[purple!40,fill=purple!2,rounded corners=3mm,line width=1pt] (9.2,8.9) rectangle(35.8,26.35);
\fill[purple!10,rounded corners=3mm] (9.2,25.0) rectangle(35.8,26.35);
\fill[purple!10] (9.2,25.0) rectangle(35.8,25.45);
\node[anchor=west,text=purple,font=\fontsize{17}{22}\selectfont\bfseries] at (9.65,25.67)
  {04\quad 误差传播与联合估计};
\node[anchor=east,text=purple,font=\fontsize{11}{15}\selectfont] at (35.30,25.67)
  {保留高程、反照率、位姿与基准的相关性};

\foreach \xa/\xb in {9.6/22.15,22.85/35.4}{
  \draw[line,fill=white,rounded corners=2mm] (\xa,17.15) rectangle(\xb,24.55);
  \draw[line,fill=white,rounded corners=2mm] (\xa,9.45) rectangle(\xb,16.45);
}

% A: state, observation and noise model.
\node[subhead] at (10.0,23.99) {A\quad 建立联合状态与观测误差};
\node[eq] at (10.05,23.08)
  {$\boldsymbol\xi=(\mathbf h^T,\boldsymbol\eta^T)^T,\qquad
    \mathbf r(\boldsymbol\xi)=\mathbf z-\mathbf f(\boldsymbol\xi)$};
\node[note,text width=11.6cm] at (10.05,22.45)
  {$\boldsymbol\xi$：全部待估参数；$\mathbf h$：所有网格点的高程。\\
   $\boldsymbol\eta$：反照率、归一化系数，以及不确定的位姿、基准等。\\
   $\mathbf z$：原始像点、亮度和控制测量；$\mathbf r$：观测减预测的残差。\\
   $\mathbf f$：由 SPC 的投影、反射与控制测量公式生成预测值。};
\draw[line] (10.05,19.98)--(21.70,19.98);
\node[note,text width=11.6cm] at (10.05,19.56)
  {\textbf{观测噪声协方差 $\boldsymbol\Sigma$}\\
   对角项：各像点、亮度与控制测量的噪声方差。\\
   非对角项：未通过共同参数解释的相关测量噪声。\\
   噪声尺度由标定或重复观测确定；共享误差应统一建模。};

% B: MAP/weighted least-squares estimate and local normal equations.
\node[subhead] at (23.25,23.99) {B\quad 联合估计：用同一组观测求解参数};
\node[eq,font=\fontsize{12}{18}\selectfont] at (23.3,22.95)
  {$\displaystyle \hat{\boldsymbol\xi}=\arg\min_{\boldsymbol\xi}
    \left[\mathbf r^T\boldsymbol\Sigma^{-1}\mathbf r
    +(\boldsymbol\xi-\boldsymbol\xi_0)^T
    \boldsymbol\Lambda_0(\boldsymbol\xi-\boldsymbol\xi_0)\right]$};
\node[note,text width=11.5cm] at (23.3,22.30)
  {$\boldsymbol\xi_0$：先验均值；$\boldsymbol\Lambda_0$：有统计依据的先验信息。\\
   $\arg\min$ 表示取使加权观测误差与先验偏差最小的参数。};
\node[eq] at (23.3,20.76)
  {$\displaystyle \mathbf J=\frac{\partial\mathbf f}{\partial\boldsymbol\xi},
   \qquad
   \mathbf F=\mathbf J^T\boldsymbol\Sigma^{-1}\mathbf J+\boldsymbol\Lambda_0$};
\node[note,text width=11.5cm] at (23.3,20.15)
  {$\mathbf J$：预测观测对参数的变化率（雅可比）。\\
   $\mathbf F$：联合信息矩阵，汇总各方向受到的约束。};
\node[eq,font=\fontsize{12}{18}\selectfont] at (23.3,18.63)
  {$\mathbf F\,\delta\boldsymbol\xi
   =\mathbf J^T\boldsymbol\Sigma^{-1}\mathbf r
    -\boldsymbol\Lambda_0(\boldsymbol\xi-\boldsymbol\xi_0),
   \quad \boldsymbol\xi\leftarrow\boldsymbol\xi+\delta\boldsymbol\xi$};
\node[caption,text width=11.5cm] at (29.13,17.65)
  {$\delta\boldsymbol\xi$ 为本轮修正；更新参数、重新线性化并迭代至收敛。};

% C: full covariance, marginalization and the importance of cross terms.
\node[subhead] at (10.0,15.91) {C\quad 从联合信息传播到高程协方差};
\node[eq] at (10.05,14.83)
  {$\displaystyle \mathbf C\approx\mathbf F^{-1}
    =\begin{bmatrix}
      \mathbf C_{hh}&\mathbf C_{h\eta}\\
      \mathbf C_{\eta h}&\mathbf C_{\eta\eta}
     \end{bmatrix}$};
\node[note,text width=11.6cm] at (10.05,13.98)
  {$\mathbf C$：收敛解附近的联合协方差。\\
   $\mathbf C_{hh}$：高程协方差；$\mathbf C_{\eta\eta}$：其余参数协方差。\\
   $\mathbf C_{h\eta},\mathbf C_{\eta h}$：高程与其余参数的交叉协方差。};
\node[eq,font=\fontsize{13}{19}\selectfont] at (10.05,11.82)
  {$\displaystyle \mathbf C_{hh}\approx
    \left(\mathbf F_{hh}-\mathbf F_{h\eta}
      \mathbf F_{\eta\eta}^{-1}\mathbf F_{\eta h}\right)^{-1}$};
\node[note,text width=11.6cm] at (10.05,11.18)
  {$\mathbf F$ 的下标同样表示高程与其余参数对应的分块。\\
   该式消去其他参数，同时保留其不确定性对高程的影响。\\
   \textbf{直接取 $\mathbf F_{hh}^{-1}$ 会漏掉这种耦合。}};

% D: selected marginal height uncertainty plus visual examples.
\node[subhead] at (23.25,15.91) {D\quad 得到指定点的高程不确定度};
\node[eq] at (23.3,14.95)
  {$\displaystyle \sigma_{h_i}
    =\sqrt{\mathbf e_i^T\mathbf C\mathbf e_i},
   \qquad
   \hat h_i\pm1.96\,\sigma_{h_i}$};
\node[note,text width=11.5cm] at (23.3,14.32)
  {$i$ 是目标点编号；$\mathbf e_i$ 仅在该高程参数位置取 1，其余为 0。\\
   $\sigma_{h_i}$ 为高程标准不确定度（米）；右式为近似高斯的 95\% 区间。};
\begin{scope}[shift={(23.5,10.43)},scale=.69]\UncertaintyMap\end{scope}
\begin{scope}[shift={(27.10,10.60)},scale=1.0]\CorrelationMatrix\end{scope}
\node[caption,font=\fontsize{9.5}{13}\selectfont] at (24.64,10.03) {逐点标准差图};
\node[caption,font=\fontsize{9.5}{13}\selectfont] at (28.12,10.03) {参数相关性};
\node[note,text width=4.8cm,font=\fontsize{10}{13.5}\selectfont] at (30.13,12.70)
  {\textbf{估计结果示例}\\[4pt]
   高程估计：$1.20$ 米\\
   标准不确定度：$0.10$ 米\\
   约 95\% 区间：\\
   $[1.004,\ 1.396]$ 米\\[3pt]
   \textcolor{muted}{示例数值，非实测精度。}};

% Explicit order A -> B -> C -> D.
\draw[flow,draw=purple] (22.23,21.12)--(22.77,21.12);
\draw[flow,draw=purple,rounded corners=2mm] (29.12,17.11)--(29.12,16.82)--(15.88,16.82)--(15.88,16.49);
\draw[flow,draw=purple] (22.23,12.85)--(22.77,12.85);

% What must persist for forecasting.
\draw[flow,draw=purple] (22.50,8.87)--(22.50,8.50);
\fill[purple!8,rounded corners=2mm] (0,7.03) rectangle(35.8,8.43);
\node[anchor=west,font=\fontsize{12}{16}\selectfont\bfseries,text=purple] at (.45,7.95)
  {最终保存};
\node[anchor=west,font=\fontsize{11}{16}\selectfont] at (3.1,7.95)
  {当前估计 $\hat{\boldsymbol\xi}$；稀疏信息矩阵 $\mathbf F$ 或联合协方差 $\mathbf C$；变量索引与线性化位置；噪声、先验及高程基准。};
\node[anchor=west,font=\fontsize{10.5}{15}\selectfont,text=muted] at (3.1,7.40)
  {逐点 $\sigma_{h_i}$ 适合显示，但不足以预测补拍收益；空间约束和参数间的相关性必须保留。};

% Keep forecast as a short downstream application of the main estimation panel.
\fill[blue!5,rounded corners=3mm] (0,2.26) rectangle(8.5,6.52);
\node[anchor=west,font=\fontsize{13}{17}\selectfont\bfseries,text=blue] at (.45,5.95)
  {下一次拍摄：已知相机参数};
\node[note,text width=7.45cm] at (.5,5.37)
  {补充太阳方向或时刻、曝光条件，\\
   预测可见性、像点、亮度与噪声。\\[6pt]
   $\mathbf J_a$：候选 $a$ 的观测雅可比。\\
   $\mathbf N_a$：候选观测的噪声协方差。\\
   $a$ 是拍摄方案编号。};

\fill[purple!5,rounded corners=3mm] (9.2,2.26) rectangle(26.7,6.52);
\node[anchor=west,font=\fontsize{13}{17}\selectfont\bfseries,text=purple] at (9.65,5.95)
  {用新增观测信息预测高程方差下降};
\node[eq,font=\fontsize{17.28}{23}\selectfont] at (9.8,4.92)
  {$\displaystyle \mathbf C^+(a)\approx
   \left(\mathbf F+\mathbf J_a^T\mathbf N_a^{-1}\mathbf J_a\right)^{-1}$};
\node[eq] at (9.8,3.72)
  {$\displaystyle \Delta\sigma_{h_i}^2(a)
    =\mathbf e_i^T\bigl(\mathbf C-\mathbf C^+(a)\bigr)\mathbf e_i$};
\node[note,text width=16.3cm,text=muted,font=\fontsize{10}{14}\selectfont] at (9.8,3.10)
  {$\mathbf C^+(a)$：加入候选观测后的预测协方差；$\Delta\sigma_{h_i}^2(a)$：目标高程的预测方差减少量。\\
   采用局部线性近似；给定共同参数后，新旧观测噪声独立。};

\fill[teal!6,rounded corners=3mm] (27.3,2.26) rectangle(35.8,6.52);
\node[anchor=west,font=\fontsize{13}{17}\selectfont\bfseries,text=teal] at (27.75,5.95)
  {预测收益，用于候选排序};
\node[anchor=west,font=\fontsize{10.5}{15}\selectfont] at (27.8,5.13) {拍摄前};
\fill[blue!65,rounded corners=1mm] (29.55,4.90) rectangle(34.40,5.30);
\node[anchor=east,font=\fontsize{11}{14}\selectfont,text=white] at (34.27,5.10) {0.10 m};
\node[anchor=west,font=\fontsize{10.5}{15}\selectfont] at (27.8,4.30) {预测后};
\fill[teal,rounded corners=1mm] (29.55,4.07) rectangle(32.46,4.47);
\node[anchor=east,font=\fontsize{11}{14}\selectfont,text=white] at (32.35,4.27) {0.06 m};
\node[caption,text width=7.4cm] at (31.55,3.48)
  {同一目标点的补拍收益示意\\标准差单位：米（m）；数值为示意};
\node[caption,text width=7.5cm] at (31.55,2.70)
  {实际拍摄后，重新求解 SPC 并更新统计状态。};
\draw[flow] (8.56,4.40)--(9.12,4.40);
\draw[flow] (26.76,4.40)--(27.23,4.40);

% All notation and interpretation limits, without introducing unexplained functions.
\draw[line] (0,1.85)--(35.8,1.85);
\node[note,text width=35.2cm,font=\fontsize{9.5}{13.5}\selectfont,text=muted] at (.1,1.45)
  {\textbf{符号：}粗体为向量或矩阵；$T$ 为转置，$^{-1}$ 为求逆，$\partial$ 为偏导数，
   $\hat{\ }$ 表示估计，$\delta$ 表示修正，$\leftarrow$ 表示更新，$\sqrt{\ }$ 表示平方根，$\Delta$ 表示减少量。\\
   \textbf{适用条件：}基准与尺度自由度已处理，相关矩阵可逆，噪声尺度可信；协方差采用收敛解附近的局部高斯近似。
   区间未计入未建模的系统偏差；同一观测不能重复计入，数值阻尼不能作为物理信息。\\
   图片与数值为合成教学示例。高程相对于固定参考面；参考面或变换误差须传播到任务高程。
   新图像引入待估位姿或亮度系数时，先扩充状态与先验再预测。};
\end{tikzpicture}
